File Information
Collection: Qavant — API Tests
Environment: Qavant API — restful-booker
Collection Description
REST API test suite by Peter (Qavant). Folders 1–4 exercise full CRUD + auth against restful-booker (https://restful-booker.herokuapp.com), a public practice API that is deliberately buggy — quirks are documented as inline NOTE comments so the expected-vs-conventional behaviour is explicit. Folder 5 is a production contract check against the live qavant.dev: status codes, security headers and cache policy — the HTTP layer beneath the UI suite.

                <h3 data-i18n="srv.s1.title">Test Automation Framework</h3>
                <p data-i18n="srv.s1.desc">Postavím ti udržiavateľný framework od nuly — Page Object Model, čisté wait stratégie, integrácia do CI a reporty, ktoré nikoho nebolia pri code review.</p>

                  <li>Selenium / Playwright</li>

                </div>
                <h3 data-i18n="srv.s2.title">API & Regression Testing</h3>
                <p data-i18n="srv.s2.desc">REST Assured, kontraktné testy a regresia, ktorá zachytí problém skôr než user. Zameranie na fintech flow — platby, autentifikácia, 2FA, edge cases.</p>
                <ul>
                  <li>REST Assured / Postman</li>
                  <li>Contract testing</li>
                  <li>Test data management</li>
                </ul>
              </div>

                <h3 data-i18n="srv.s3.title">QA Audit & Consulting</h3>
                <p data-i18n="srv.s3.desc">Pozriem sa na tvoj test setup, nájdem flaky testy, ukážem kde sa pozerá nesprávnym smerom a pripravím plán, ako z toho von bez prerábania všetkého.</p>
                <ul>
                  <li>Test strategy review</li>
                  <li>Flaky tests audit</li>
                  <li>QA process design</li>

                  <svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="1.6"><rect x="3" y="3" width="7" height="7" rx="1"/><rect x="14" y="3" width="7" height="7" rx="1"/><rect x="3" y="14" width="7" height="7" rx="1"/><rect x="14" y="14" width="7" height="7" rx="1"/></svg>
                </span>
                <h4 data-i18n="show.m1.title">Page Object Model</h4>
                <p data-i18n="show.m1.desc">Každá stránka aplikácie je samostatná trieda. Testy nikdy nehľadajú elementy priamo — to je zodpovednosť page objektu.</p>
              </div>
              <div class="meta-card">
                <span class="icon">
                  <svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="1.6"><path d="M12 2v4M12 18v4M4.93 4.93l2.83 2.83M16.24 16.24l2.83 2.83M2 12h4M18 12h4M4.93 19.07l2.83-2.83M16.24 7.76l2.83-2.83"/></svg>
                </span>
                <h4 data-i18n="show.m2.title">Explicit Waits Only</h4>
                <p data-i18n="show.m2.desc">Žiadne Thread.sleep(). Každá akcia čaká presne na to, čo potrebuje. Výsledok: testy bežia rýchlejšie a nepadajú náhodne.</p>
              </div>
              <div class="meta-card">
                <span class="icon">
                  <svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="1.6"><path d="M9 11l3 3L22 4"/><path d="M21 12v7a2 2 0 01-2 2H5a2 2 0 01-2-2V5a2 2 0 012-2h11"/></svg>
                </span>
                <h4 data-i18n="show.m3.title">CI-First Architecture</h4>
                <p data-i18n="show.m3.desc">Framework je navrhnutý ako prvé pre Jenkins/GitLab CI, nie pre lokálny vývoj. Allure reporty, paralelné spúšťanie, retry logika.</p>
              </div>
            </div>
        
            <div class="tech-tags reveal">
              <span class="tech-tag">Java 17</span>
              <span class="tech-tag">Selenium 4.x</span>
              <span class="tech-tag">TestNG</span>
              <span class="tech-tag">Maven</span>
              <span class="tech-tag">Page Factory</span>
              <span class="tech-tag">Allure</span>
              <span class="tech-tag">WebDriverManager</span>
              <span class="tech-tag">Log4j2</span>
              <span class="tech-tag">Jenkins / GitLab CI</span>
            </div>
          </div>
        </section>

        <section class="mission zone-light" id="mission">
          <div class="container">
            <div class="mission-inner reveal">
              <div class="mission-kicker"><span data-i18n="mission.kicker">Manifesto</span></div>
        
              <p class="mission-statement">
                <span data-i18n="mission.s1">QA pre mňa nie je o automatizácii a nástrojoch. Tie sa menia.</span>
                <em data-i18n="mission.s2">Je o zodpovednosti voči používateľovi a rešpekte k práci tímu.</em>
                <span data-i18n="mission.s3">Som tu na to, aby som zložité veci robil spoľahlivými.</span>
              </p>
        
              <div class="mission-sig">
                <span data-i18n="mission.sig">Peter · Qavant</span>
              </div>
        
              <div class="principles">
                <div class="principle">
                  <div class="principle-num">01</div>
                  <h5 data-i18n="mission.p1.t">Detail je profesia</h5>
                  <p data-i18n="mission.p1.d">Každý prehliadnutý bug je niekoho zlý deň. Beriem to osobne.</p>
                </div>
                <div class="principle">
                  <div class="principle-num">02</div>
                  <h5 data-i18n="mission.p2.t">Žiadny vendor lock-in</h5>
                  <p data-i18n="mission.p2.d">Tvoj framework, tvoj kód. Ak odídem, tím beží ďalej bez výpadku.</p>
                </div>
                <div class="principle">
                  <div class="principle-num">03</div>
                  <h5 data-i18n="mission.p3.t">Predvídateľnosť pred rýchlosťou</h5>
                  <p data-i18n="mission.p3.d">Radšej pomalšie a stabilne, než rýchlo a flaky. Z dlhého pohľadu sa to vždy oplatí.</p>
                </div>
                <div class="principle">
                  <div class="principle-num">04</div>
                  <h5 data-i18n="mission.p4.t">Honest work, honest pricing</h5>
                  <p data-i18n="mission.p4.d">Fixné ceny, jasný rozsah, žiadne hodinové faktúry s prekvapeniami na konci mesiaca.</p>
                </div>
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>
        </section>

        <section id="process">
          <div class="container">
            <div class="section-head reveal">
              <div class="section-num">[ 04 / <span data-i18n="proc.kicker">Spolupráca</span> ]</div>
              <h2><span data-i18n="proc.t1">Bez prekvapení.</span> <em data-i18n="proc.t2">Predvídateľný proces.</em></h2>
              <p style="color:var(--ink-2);font-size:17px;line-height:1.6;margin-top:24px;max-width:640px;" data-i18n="proc.intro">
                Štyri jasné kroky od prvého emailu po odovzdanie. Vieš čo sa kedy deje, ja viem čo sa odo mňa očakáva. Žiadny black-box freelancer.
              </p>
            </div>
        
            <div class="process-grid reveal" id="processGrid">
              <div class="process-step">
                <div class="step-number"><span class="num">01</span><span class="of">/ 04</span></div>
                <h4 data-i18n="proc.s1.title">Discovery</h4>
                <p data-i18n="proc.s1.desc">30-minútový hovor o projekte. Aký stack, aká bolesť, aký cieľ. Žiadny záväzok ani faktúra — chcem vedieť či si dokážeme pomôcť.</p>
                <div class="duration" data-i18n="proc.s1.dur">30 min · zadarmo</div>
              </div>
        
              <div class="process-step">
                <div class="step-number"><span class="num">02</span><span class="of">/ 04</span></div>
                <h4 data-i18n="proc.s2.title">Audit & Plan</h4>
                <p data-i18n="proc.s2.desc">Pozriem si váš kód a CI. Pošlem písomný audit s nálezmi a plán implementácie — rozsah, timeline, fixná cena.</p>
                <div class="duration" data-i18n="proc.s2.dur">3–5 dní</div>
              </div>
        
              <div class="process-step">
                <div class="step-number"><span class="num">03</span><span class="of">/ 04</span></div>
                <h4 data-i18n="proc.s3.title">Build</h4>
                <p data-i18n="proc.s3.desc">Stavba frameworku, integrácia do CI, písanie prvých testov. Týždenné check-iny, demo každý piatok, kód v repe od dňa jedna.</p>
                <div class="duration" data-i18n="proc.s3.dur">2–6 týždňov</div>
              </div>
        
              <div class="process-step">
                <div class="step-number"><span class="num">04</span><span class="of">/ 04</span></div>
                <h4 data-i18n="proc.s4.title">Handover</h4>
                <p data-i18n="proc.s4.desc">Dokumentácia, video walkthrough, onboarding tvojho tímu. 30 dní podpory zadarmo. Tvoj framework, tvoj kód, žiadny vendor lock-in.</p>
                <div class="duration" data-i18n="proc.s4.dur">1 týždeň + 30 dní</div>
              </div>
            </div>
        
            <div class="mid-cta mid-cta-dark reveal">
              <div class="mid-cta-inner">

                  <p class="mid-cta-desc" data-i18n="midcta2.desc">Posledný krok je jednoduchý — napíš mi pár riadkov o tvojom projekte.</p>

        function applyLang(lang){
          document.documentElement.lang = lang;
          document.querySelectorAll('[data-i18n]').forEach(el=>{
            const key = el.getAttribute('data-i18n');
            const val = dict[lang]?.[key];
            if(val !== undefined) el.textContent = val;
          });
          document.querySelectorAll('.lang-switch button').forEach(b=>{
            const active = b.dataset.lang === lang;
            b.classList.toggle('active', active);
            b.setAttribute('aria-pressed', active ? 'true':'false');
          });
          try{ localStorage.setItem('qavant.lang', lang); }catch(e){}
        }
        document.querySelectorAll('.lang-switch button').forEach(b=>{
          b.addEventListener('click', ()=> applyLang(b.dataset.lang));
        });
        let initLang = 'sk';
        try{
          const saved = localStorage.getItem('qavant.lang');
          if(saved && dict[saved]) initLang = saved;
          else {
            const browserLang = (navigator.language||'').toLowerCase();
            if(browserLang.startsWith('de')) initLang = 'de';
            else if(browserLang.startsWith('en')) initLang = 'en';
          }
        }catch(e){}
        applyLang(initLang);
        
        window.addEventListener('load', ()=>{
          // CURTAIN: OCP-style letter assembly → BOOT SEQUENCE → TERMINAL
          // Shortened from 2.6s → 1.4s for faster First Contentful Paint
          // Letter assembly still completes but with tighter pacing
        
          // Stage 1: Curtain holds for 1.4s — quick reveal
          setTimeout(()=>{
            document.getElementById('curtain').classList.add('gone');
          }, 1400);
        
          // Stage 1.5: Header logo assembly starts as curtain leaves
          setTimeout(()=>{
            const navLogo = document.getElementById('navLogo');
            if(navLogo) navLogo.classList.add('assembled');
          }, 1600);
        
          // Stage 2: Boot sequence + hero title reveal
          setTimeout(()=>{
            document.getElementById('boot').classList.add('gone');
            document.getElementById('heroTitle').classList.add('shown');
            setTimeout(runTerminal, 400);
          }, 2400);
        });
        
        // Safety net: if anything fails, ensure curtain is removed by 4s
        setTimeout(()=>{
          const curtain = document.getElementById('curtain');
          if(curtain && !curtain.classList.contains('gone')){
            curtain.classList.add('gone');
          }
          // Force hero title to shown state if not already
          const ht = document.getElementById('heroTitle');
          if(ht && !ht.classList.contains('shown')){
            ht.classList.add('shown');
          }
          // Force logo assembled
          const navLogo = document.getElementById('navLogo');
          if(navLogo && !navLogo.classList.contains('assembled')){
            navLogo.classList.add('assembled');
          }
          // also remove curtain from DOM after 6s to free up memory
          setTimeout(()=>{
            const c = document.getElementById('curtain');
            if(c) c.remove();
            const b = document.getElementById('boot');
            if(b && b.classList.contains('gone')) b.remove();
          }, 6000);
        }, 4000);
        
        // ============== SUITE FACTS — SINGLE SOURCE OF TRUTH ==============

        // A published status.json older than this is shown as stale, not as fresh CI data.

        function runTerminal(){
          const body = document.getElementById('termBody');
          body.innerHTML = '';
          let delay = 0;
          termLines.forEach(line=>{
            delay += line.d;
            setTimeout(()=>{
              const el = document.createElement('span');
              el.className = 'term-line';
              el.innerHTML = line.t;
              body.appendChild(el);
            }, delay);
          });
          const term = document.getElementById('terminal');
          if(!term._bound){
            term._bound = true;
            term.addEventListener('mouseenter', ()=>{
              if(term._busy) return;
              term._busy = true;
              runTerminal();
              setTimeout(()=> term._busy = false, 4500);
            });
          }
        }
        
        const io = new IntersectionObserver(entries=>{
          entries.forEach(e=>{
            if(e.isIntersecting){
              e.target.classList.add('in');
              io.unobserve(e.target);
            }
          });
        }, { threshold: 0.12 });
        document.querySelectorAll('.reveal').forEach(el=> io.observe(el));
        
        const counterIO = new IntersectionObserver(entries=>{
          entries.forEach(e=>{
            if(e.isIntersecting){
              const el = e.target;
              const target = parseInt(el.dataset.count, 10);
              const suffix = el.dataset.suffix || '';
              const dur = 1400;
              const start = performance.now();
              const animate = (t)=>{
                const p = Math.min(1, (t-start)/dur);
                const eased = 1 - Math.pow(1-p, 3);
                const val = Math.round(target * eased);
                el.innerHTML = val + (suffix ? `<em>${suffix}</em>` : '');
                if(p < 1) requestAnimationFrame(animate);
              };
              requestAnimationFrame(animate);
              counterIO.unobserve(el);
            }
          });
        }, { threshold: 0.5 });
        document.querySelectorAll('[data-count]').forEach(el=> counterIO.observe(el));
        
        // Single source of truth for the "calm mode" — respects the OS-level
        // "Reduce Motion" / "Reduce animations" accessibility setting. On macOS:
        // System Settings → Accessibility → Display → Reduce motion.
        // This is the recommended switch when running over DisplayLink or on a
        // fanless machine, where continuous repaints are expensive.
        const reduceMotion = matchMedia('(prefers-reduced-motion:reduce)').matches;
        
        // PERFORMANCE-FIRST BUILD: all continuous mouse/scroll-reactive effects
        // (custom cursor, magnetic typography, magnetic buttons, hero aura, card/step
        // spotlights, hero 3D canvas, terminal parallax) are disabled for maximum
        // fluidity. Flip PERF_MAX to false to restore the full decorative experience.
        const PERF_MAX = true;
        const calm = reduceMotion || PERF_MAX;
        
        const dot = document.getElementById('cursorDot');
        const ring = document.getElementById('cursorRing');
        if(dot && ring && !calm){
        let mx=0, my=0, rx=0, ry=0;
        let ringRaf = null;
        document.addEventListener('mousemove', e=>{
          mx = e.clientX; my = e.clientY;
          dot.style.transform = `translate(${mx}px, ${my}px) translate(-50%, -50%)`;
          if(!ringRaf) ringRaf = requestAnimationFrame(loop);
        });
        function loop(){
          rx += (mx - rx) * 0.18;
          ry += (my - ry) * 0.18;
          ring.style.transform = `translate(${rx}px, ${ry}px) translate(-50%, -50%)`;
          // Stop the loop once the ring has caught up to the cursor; restart on next move.
          if(Math.abs(mx - rx) > 0.1 || Math.abs(my - ry) > 0.1){
            ringRaf = requestAnimationFrame(loop);
          } else {
            ringRaf = null;
          }
        }
        document.querySelectorAll('[data-cursor="hover"]').forEach(el=>{
          el.addEventListener('mouseenter', ()=>{
            ring.classList.add('is-hover');
            dot.classList.add('is-hover');
          });
          el.addEventListener('mouseleave', ()=>{
            ring.classList.remove('is-hover');
            dot.classList.remove('is-hover');
          });
        });
        }
        
        if(!calm) document.querySelectorAll('.service, #contactWrap').forEach(card=>{
          card.addEventListener('mousemove', e=>{
            const r = card.getBoundingClientRect();
            card.style.setProperty('--mx', (e.clientX - r.left) + 'px');
            card.style.setProperty('--my', (e.clientY - r.top) + 'px');
          });
        });
        
        // ============== HERO AURA (mouse-reactive gradient) ==============
        const heroAura = document.getElementById('heroAura');
        const isCoarsePtr = matchMedia('(pointer:coarse)').matches;
        if(heroAura && !isCoarsePtr && !calm){
          let auraX = 50, auraY = 40, targetX = 50, targetY = 40;
          let auraRaf = null;
          document.addEventListener('mousemove', e=>{
            targetX = (e.clientX / window.innerWidth) * 100;
            targetY = (e.clientY / window.innerHeight) * 100;
            if(!auraRaf) auraRaf = requestAnimationFrame(auraLoop);
          });
          function auraLoop(){
            auraX += (targetX - auraX) * 0.06;
            auraY += (targetY - auraY) * 0.06;
            heroAura.style.setProperty('--hx', auraX + '%');
            heroAura.style.setProperty('--hy', auraY + '%');
            // Idle once the aura has settled; restart on next mouse move.
            if(Math.abs(targetX - auraX) > 0.1 || Math.abs(targetY - auraY) > 0.1){
              auraRaf = requestAnimationFrame(auraLoop);
            } else {
              auraRaf = null;
            }
          }
        }
        
        // ============== PROCESS STEP SPOTLIGHT ==============
        if(!calm) document.querySelectorAll('.process-step').forEach(step=>{
          step.addEventListener('mousemove', e=>{
            const r = step.getBoundingClientRect();
            step.style.setProperty('--mx', (e.clientX - r.left) + 'px');
            step.style.setProperty('--my', (e.clientY - r.top) + 'px');
          });
        });
        
        // ============== LIVE METRICS WIDGET ==============
        // To use REAL data later, set USE_LIVE_METRICS = true and configure the source.
        // Both suites publish status.json from their own CI runs (see fetchStatus below).
        const USE_LIVE_METRICS = true;
        // Published by the qavant-tests GitHub Actions run after each test run.
        const METRICS_URL = 'https://raw.githubusercontent.com/peterolah-qa/qavant-tests/main/status.json';
        const API_METRICS_URL = 'https://raw.githubusercontent.com/peterolah-qa/qavant-api-tests/main/status.json';
        
        // Fallbacks are derived from SUITE — no separate numbers to drift out of sync.
        const uiFallback  = { testsRun: SUITE.ui.total,  testsTotal: SUITE.ui.total,
                              passRate: SUITE.ui.passRate,  totalRuns: SUITE.ui.runs,
                              live: false, stale: false, lastRun: null };
        const apiFallback = { testsRun: SUITE.api.total, testsTotal: SUITE.api.total,
                              passRate: SUITE.api.passRate, totalRuns: SUITE.api.runs,
                              live: false, stale: false, lastRun: null };
        
        async function fetchStatus(url, fallback){
          if(!USE_LIVE_METRICS) return { ...fallback };
          try{
            const r = await fetch(url, { cache: 'no-store' });
            if(!r.ok) throw new Error('HTTP ' + r.status);
            const d = await r.json();
            const lastRun = d.lastRun ? new Date(d.lastRun) : null;
            const ageDays = (lastRun && !isNaN(lastRun))
              ? (Date.now() - lastRun.getTime()) / 86400000
              : null;
            return {
              testsRun:   Number.isFinite(d.passed)   ? d.passed   : fallback.testsRun,
              testsTotal: Number.isFinite(d.total)    ? d.total    : fallback.testsTotal,
              passRate:   Number.isFinite(d.passRate) ? d.passRate : fallback.passRate,
              totalRuns:  Number.isFinite(d.runs)     ? d.runs     : fallback.totalRuns,
              live: true,
              // an old status.json is real data, but it is not a fresh run — say so
              stale: ageDays === null || ageDays > STALE_AFTER_DAYS,
              lastRun
            };
          }catch(e){
            // graceful fallback — the widget still works; DEMO stays for this source
            return { ...fallback };
          }
        }
        
        // Render the /total denominators before any fetch, so the number never
        // jumps from a placeholder to the real value mid-animation.
        function paintTotals(){
          const ui = document.getElementById('metricTestsTotal');
          const api = document.getElementById('metricApiTotal');
          if(ui)  ui.textContent  = '/' + SUITE.ui.total;
          if(api) api.textContent = '/' + SUITE.api.total;
        }
        paintTotals();
        
        function paintAge(el, src){
          if(!el) return;
          if(!src.live || !src.lastRun){ el.textContent = ''; return; }
          const d = src.lastRun;
          const label = d.toLocaleDateString(document.documentElement.lang || 'sk', { day:'numeric', month:'short' });
          el.textContent = ' · ' + label;
          el.classList.toggle('stale', !!src.stale);
          el.title = d.toLocaleString();
        }
        
        function animateMetric(el, target, opts={}){
          const dur = opts.duration || 1400;
          const decimals = opts.decimals || 0;
          const start = performance.now();
          const animate = (t)=>{
            const p = Math.min(1, (t-start)/dur);
            const eased = 1 - Math.pow(1-p, 3);
            const val = (target * eased).toFixed(decimals);
            el(val);
            if(p < 1) requestAnimationFrame(animate);
          };
          requestAnimationFrame(animate);
        }
        
        const metricsIO = new IntersectionObserver(async entries=>{
          for(const e of entries){
            if(e.isIntersecting){
              metricsIO.unobserve(e.target);
              // both suites publish the same status.json contract from their own CI
              const [ui, api] = await Promise.all([
                fetchStatus(METRICS_URL, uiFallback),
                fetchStatus(API_METRICS_URL, apiFallback),
              ]);
        
              animateMetric(v=>{
                document.getElementById('metricTests').innerHTML =
                  `${Math.round(v)}<span class="unit" id="metricTestsTotal">/${ui.testsTotal}</span>`;
              }, ui.testsRun);
              animateMetric(v=>{
                document.getElementById('metricApi').innerHTML =
                  `${Math.round(v)}<span class="unit" id="metricApiTotal">/${api.testsTotal}</span>`;
              }, api.testsRun);
        
              document.getElementById('metricUiMeta').textContent  = `${ui.passRate}% · ${ui.totalRuns} `;
              document.getElementById('metricApiMeta').textContent = `${api.passRate}% · ${api.totalRuns} `;
              paintAge(document.getElementById('metricUiAge'),  ui);
              paintAge(document.getElementById('metricApiAge'), api);
        
              // drop DEMO per source — only a fresh CI run loses the tag; stale data keeps it
              if(ui.live  && !ui.stale)  document.querySelectorAll('.demo-tag.demo-ui').forEach(t=> t.style.display='none');
              if(api.live && !api.stale) document.querySelectorAll('.demo-tag.demo-api').forEach(t=> t.style.display='none');
            }
          }
        }, { threshold: 0.3 });
        const metricsEl = document.getElementById('liveMetrics');
        if(metricsEl) metricsIO.observe(metricsEl);

                  ["    screenshot: ", "str", "'only-on-failure'", ","],
                  ["    video: ", "str", "'retain-on-failure'", ","],
                  ["    actionTimeout: ", "num", "10_000", ","],
                  ["    navigationTimeout: ", "num", "30_000", ","],
                  ["  },"],
                  [],
                  ["  projects: ["],
                  ["    { name: ", "str", "'chromium'", ", use: { ...devices[", "str", "'Desktop Chrome'", "] } },"],
                  ["    { name: ", "str", "'firefox'", ",  use: { ...devices[", "str", "'Desktop Firefox'", "] } },"],
                  ["    { name: ", "str", "'webkit'", ",   use: { ...devices[", "str", "'Desktop Safari'", "] } },"],
                  ["    { name: ", "str", "'mobile-chrome'", ", use: { ...devices[", "str", "'Pixel 5'", "] } },"],
                  ["    { name: ", "str", "'mobile-safari'", ", use: { ...devices[", "str", "'iPhone 13'", "] } },"],
                  ["  ],"],
                  ["});"]
                ]
              }
            }
          }
        };
        
        let activeFramework = 'selenium';
        let activeFile = frameworks.selenium.defaultFile;
        
        function renderTree(fwKey){
          const fw = frameworks[fwKey];
          const tree = document.getElementById('ideTree');
          let html = '';
          fw.tree.forEach(section => {
            html += '<div class="tree-section">';
            if(section.label) html += `<div class="tree-label">${section.label}</div>`;
            html += `<div class="tree-folder">
              <svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2"><path d="M3 7v10a2 2 0 002 2h14a2 2 0 002-2V9a2 2 0 00-2-2h-7l-2-2H5a2 2 0 00-2 2z"/></svg>
              <span>${section.folder}</span>
            </div>`;
            section.files.forEach(file => {
              const isActive = file.key === activeFile ? 'active' : '';
              const isLocked = fw.files[file.key].locked;
              html += `<div class="tree-file ${isActive}" data-file="${file.key}" data-cursor="hover">
                <svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2"><path d="M14 2H6a2 2 0 00-2 2v16a2 2 0 002 2h12a2 2 0 002-2V8z"/><path d="M14 2v6h6"/></svg>
                <span>${file.name}</span>
                ${isLocked ? '<svg style="margin-left:auto;width:11px;height:11px;color:var(--accent-3);" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2"><rect x="3" y="11" width="18" height="11" rx="2"/><path d="M7 11V7a5 5 0 0110 0v4"/></svg>' : ''}
              </div>`;
            });
            html += '</div>';
          });
          tree.innerHTML = html;
        
          tree.querySelectorAll('.tree-file').forEach(el=>{
            el.addEventListener('click', ()=>{
              activeFile = el.dataset.file;
              tree.querySelectorAll('.tree-file').forEach(f=> f.classList.remove('active'));
              el.classList.add('active');
              renderCode(activeFramework, activeFile);
            });
          });
        }
        
        function renderCode(fwKey, fileKey){
          const fw = frameworks[fwKey];
          const file = fw.files[fileKey];
          if(!file) return;
        
          const codeEl = document.getElementById('ideCode');
          document.getElementById('activeTabName').textContent = file.name;
          document.getElementById('ideStack').textContent = fw.stack;
          const repoEl = document.getElementById('ideRepoLink');
          if(repoEl && fw.repo) repoEl.href = fw.repo;
          document.getElementById('ideProjectName').textContent = fw.project;
        
          let html = '<pre>';
          // Known syntax classes — anything else is treated as plain text
          const KNOWN_CLASSES = new Set(['kw','typ','str','com','ann','num','fn','pkg','op','var','prop','att']);
          file.code.forEach(line=>{
            html += '<div class="ln"><span class="code">';
            if(line.length === 0){
              html += '&nbsp;';
            } else {
              // Walk tokens: if current is a known class AND next exists, consume as [class, text].
              // Otherwise treat current as plain text and advance by 1.
              const escape = s => s.replace(/&/g,'&amp;').replace(/</g,'&lt;').replace(/>/g,'&gt;');
              let i = 0;
              while(i < line.length){
                const tok = line[i];
                if(typeof tok === 'string' && KNOWN_CLASSES.has(tok) && i+1 < line.length){
                  html += `<span class="${tok}">${escape(line[i+1] || '')}</span>`;
                  i += 2;
                } else {
                  html += escape(tok != null ? String(tok) : '');
                  i += 1;
                }
              }
            }
            html += '</span></div>';
          });
          html += '</pre>';
        
          // Re-attach lock overlay (it's a child of #ideCode)
          const lockHtml = `
            <div class="lock-overlay" id="lockOverlay">
              <div class="lock-card">
                <div class="lock-icon">
                  <svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2"><rect x="3" y="11" width="18" height="11" rx="2"/><path d="M7 11V7a5 5 0 0110 0v4"/></svg>
                </div>
                <h4>${dict[document.documentElement.lang]?.['lock.title'] || 'Want to see the full framework?'}</h4>
                <p>${dict[document.documentElement.lang]?.['lock.desc'] || "I share the full GitHub repository privately. Drop me a line and I'll send you access."}</p>
                <a href="#contact" class="btn btn-primary" id="requestAccessBtn" data-cursor="hover" data-magnetic>
                  <span>${dict[document.documentElement.lang]?.['lock.cta'] || 'Request full access'}</span>
                  <span class="arrow">→</span>
                </a>
              </div>
            </div>`;
        
          codeEl.innerHTML = html + lockHtml;
          codeEl.classList.toggle('locked', file.locked);
          document.getElementById('lockOverlay').classList.toggle('show', file.locked);
        
          // Re-bind cursor hover for the new button
          const reqBtn = document.getElementById('requestAccessBtn');
          if(reqBtn){
            reqBtn.addEventListener('mouseenter', ()=>{
              ring.classList.add('is-hover');
              dot.classList.add('is-hover');
            });
            reqBtn.addEventListener('mouseleave', ()=>{
              ring.classList.remove('is-hover');
              dot.classList.remove('is-hover');
            });
            reqBtn.addEventListener('click', e=>{
              // Pre-fill topic dropdown when user clicks request access
              const topicEl = document.getElementById('topic');
              if(topicEl){
                for(let i = 0; i < topicEl.options.length; i++){
                  if(topicEl.options[i].value === 'framework-access'){
                    topicEl.selectedIndex = i;
                    break;
                  }
                }
                // Pre-fill message with framework hint
                const msgEl = document.getElementById('message');
                if(msgEl && !msgEl.value){
                  const fwName = activeFramework === 'selenium' ? 'Selenium Java'
                               : activeFramework === 'api' ? 'REST API (REST Assured)'
                               : 'Playwright TS';
                  const msgPrefix = {
                    sk: `Ahoj Peter, zaujal ma tvoj ${fwName} framework. Mohol by si mi poslať prístup ku GitHub repu? Krátko o nás: `,
                    en: `Hi Peter, I'm interested in your ${fwName} framework. Could you share GitHub access? Quick context about us: `,
                    de: `Hallo Peter, ich interessiere mich für Ihr ${fwName} Framework. Könnten Sie mir GitHub-Zugang gewähren? Kurz zu uns: `
                  };
                  msgEl.value = msgPrefix[document.documentElement.lang] || msgPrefix.en;
                }
              }
            });
          }
        
          document.getElementById('lineCount').textContent =
            `${file.code.length} lines · UTF-8 · LF`;
        }
        
        function switchFramework(fwKey){
          activeFramework = fwKey;
          // Pick first non-locked file by default
          const fw = frameworks[fwKey];
          const firstFile = fw.tree[0].files[0];
          activeFile = firstFile.key;
          // Try to find a non-locked file to start with for better first impression
          for(const section of fw.tree){
            for(const f of section.files){
              if(!fw.files[f.key].locked){ activeFile = f.key; break; }
            }
            if(!fw.files[activeFile].locked) break;
          }
          renderTree(fwKey);
          renderCode(fwKey, activeFile);
        
          document.querySelectorAll('.fw-tab').forEach(t=>{
            t.classList.toggle('active', t.dataset.fw === fwKey);
          });
        }
        
        document.querySelectorAll('.fw-tab').forEach(tab=>{
          tab.addEventListener('click', ()=> switchFramework(tab.dataset.fw));
        });
        
        // Initial render
        renderTree('selenium');
        renderCode('selenium', frameworks.selenium.defaultFile);
        
        // ============== SCROLL PROGRESS BAR ==============
        const scrollProgress = document.getElementById('scrollProgress');
        let scrollIdleTimer;
        // Cache the document height; reading scrollHeight on every scroll event forces
        // a synchronous layout (reflow) and causes scroll stutter. Recompute only on resize.
        let docHeight = document.documentElement.scrollHeight - window.innerHeight;
        window.addEventListener('resize', ()=>{
          docHeight = document.documentElement.scrollHeight - window.innerHeight;
        }, { passive: true });
        window.addEventListener('scroll', ()=>{
          const progress = (window.scrollY / docHeight) * 100;
          scrollProgress.style.width = Math.min(100, progress) + '%';
          scrollProgress.classList.remove('idle');
          clearTimeout(scrollIdleTimer);
          scrollIdleTimer = setTimeout(()=> scrollProgress.classList.add('idle'), 1500);
        }, { passive: true });
        
        // ============== SECTION DIVIDERS REVEAL ==============
        const dividerIO = new IntersectionObserver(entries=>{
          entries.forEach(e=>{
            if(e.isIntersecting){
              e.target.classList.add('in');
              dividerIO.unobserve(e.target);
            }
          });
        }, { threshold: 0.5 });
        document.querySelectorAll('.divider').forEach(el=> dividerIO.observe(el));
        
        // ============== INTERLUDE WORD ANIMATION ==============
        const interludeIO = new IntersectionObserver(entries=>{
          entries.forEach(e=>{
            if(e.isIntersecting){
              e.target.classList.add('in');
              // stagger words
              const words = e.target.querySelectorAll('.word');
              words.forEach((w, i)=>{
                w.style.transitionDelay = (i * 0.15 + 0.3) + 's';
              });
              interludeIO.unobserve(e.target);
            }
          });
        }, { threshold: 0.4 });
        const interludeEl = document.getElementById('interlude');
        if(interludeEl) interludeIO.observe(interludeEl);
        
        // ============== MAGNETIC BUTTONS (spring physics) ==============
        if(!calm) document.querySelectorAll('[data-magnetic]').forEach(btn=>{
          bindMagnetic(btn);
        });
        function bindMagnetic(btn){
          if(btn._magneticBound) return;
          btn._magneticBound = true;
        
          const STRENGTH = 0.30;     // pull strength
          const MAX_OFFSET = 8;      // px cap
          const STIFFNESS = 0.12;    // spring stiffness (higher = snappier)
          const DAMPING = 0.72;      // velocity damping (lower = more bounce)
        
          // Current and target positions, plus velocity for spring
          let curX = 0, curY = 0;
          let velX = 0, velY = 0;
          let targetX = 0, targetY = 0;
          let rafId = null;
          let isHovering = false;
        
          function tick(){
            // Spring: F = -k * x, with damping
            const ax = (targetX - curX) * STIFFNESS;
            const ay = (targetY - curY) * STIFFNESS;
            velX = (velX + ax) * DAMPING;
            velY = (velY + ay) * DAMPING;
            curX += velX;
            curY += velY;
        
            // Apply (small rotation tilts toward direction of motion for tactile feel)
            const rotZ = velX * 0.3;  // sympathetic micro-rotation
            btn.style.transform = `translate3d(${curX.toFixed(2)}px, ${curY.toFixed(2)}px, 0) rotate(${rotZ.toFixed(2)}deg)`;
        
            // Inner parallax with secondary motion
            Array.from(btn.children).forEach((child, i)=>{
              const lag = 0.5 + i * 0.05; // children lag slightly behind for organic feel
              child.style.transform = `translate3d(${(curX*lag).toFixed(2)}px, ${(curY*lag).toFixed(2)}px, 0)`;
            });
        
            // Continue animation if still moving meaningfully OR still hovering
            const stillMoving = Math.abs(velX) > 0.05 || Math.abs(velY) > 0.05
                             || Math.abs(targetX - curX) > 0.1 || Math.abs(targetY - curY) > 0.1;
            if(stillMoving || isHovering){
              rafId = requestAnimationFrame(tick);
            } else {
              // Snap to zero and reset
              btn.style.transform = '';
              Array.from(btn.children).forEach(child=> child.style.transform = '');
              curX = curY = velX = velY = 0;
              rafId = null;
            }
          }
        
          btn.addEventListener('mouseenter', ()=>{
            isHovering = true;
            if(!rafId) rafId = requestAnimationFrame(tick);
          });
        
          btn.addEventListener('mousemove', (e)=>{
            const r = btn.getBoundingClientRect();
            const cx = r.left + r.width / 2;
            const cy = r.top + r.height / 2;
            const dx = (e.clientX - cx) * STRENGTH;
            const dy = (e.clientY - cy) * STRENGTH;
            targetX = Math.max(-MAX_OFFSET, Math.min(MAX_OFFSET, dx));
            targetY = Math.max(-MAX_OFFSET, Math.min(MAX_OFFSET, dy));
          });
        
          btn.addEventListener('mouseleave', ()=>{
            isHovering = false;
            targetX = 0;
            targetY = 0;
            // Spring naturally settles back to 0 with overshoot
            if(!rafId) rafId = requestAnimationFrame(tick);
          });
        }
        
        // ============== MAGNETIC TYPOGRAPHY (hero) ==============
        const heroTitle = document.getElementById('heroTitle');
        if(heroTitle && !matchMedia('(pointer:coarse)').matches && !calm){
          const words = Array.from(heroTitle.querySelectorAll('.word'));
          const STRENGTH = 14;       // max px translation
          const ROT_STRENGTH = 1.2;  // max degrees rotation
          const RADIUS = 280;        // px from word center; influence falloff
        
          // Spring state per word
          const states = words.map(()=> ({
            x:0, y:0, vx:0, vy:0, tx:0, ty:0,
            cx:0, cy:0  // word center (updated on resize)
          }));
        
          function updateCenters(){
            words.forEach((w, i)=>{
              const r = w.getBoundingClientRect();
              states[i].cx = r.left + r.width/2;
              states[i].cy = r.top + r.height/2;
            });
          }
          updateCenters();
          // Reading getBoundingClientRect for every word on every scroll event forces a
          // synchronous layout and is a major source of scroll jank. Coalesce updates to
          // at most one per frame via rAF, and skip entirely once the hero is scrolled away.
          let centersPending = false;
          function scheduleCenters(){
            if(centersPending) return;
            centersPending = true;
            requestAnimationFrame(()=>{
              centersPending = false;
              if(window.scrollY < window.innerHeight) updateCenters();
            });
          }
          window.addEventListener('resize', scheduleCenters, { passive:true });
          window.addEventListener('scroll', scheduleCenters, { passive:true });
        
          let mouseX = -9999, mouseY = -9999;
          let rafTypoId = null;
        
          document.addEventListener('mousemove', e=>{
            mouseX = e.clientX; mouseY = e.clientY;
            if(!rafTypoId) rafTypoId = requestAnimationFrame(tickTypo);
          });
        
          function tickTypo(){
            let anyMoving = false;
            words.forEach((w, i)=>{
              const s = states[i];
              const dx = mouseX - s.cx;
              const dy = mouseY - s.cy;
              const dist = Math.sqrt(dx*dx + dy*dy);
        
              // Falloff: full strength at distance 0, zero at RADIUS
              const influence = Math.max(0, 1 - dist / RADIUS);
              // Smooth falloff curve (quadratic)
              const falloff = influence * influence;
        
              s.tx = (dx / RADIUS) * STRENGTH * falloff;
              s.ty = (dy / RADIUS) * STRENGTH * falloff;
        
              // Spring step (slightly underdamped for organic feel)
              const ax = (s.tx - s.x) * 0.10;
              const ay = (s.ty - s.y) * 0.10;
              s.vx = (s.vx + ax) * 0.78;
              s.vy = (s.vy + ay) * 0.78;
              s.x += s.vx;
              s.y += s.vy;
        
              // Rotation tilts toward mouse direction
              const rot = (s.x / STRENGTH) * ROT_STRENGTH;
        
              w.style.setProperty('--tx', s.x.toFixed(2) + 'px');
              w.style.setProperty('--ty', s.y.toFixed(2) + 'px');
              w.style.setProperty('--rot', rot.toFixed(2) + 'deg');
        
              if(Math.abs(s.vx) > 0.01 || Math.abs(s.vy) > 0.01
                 || Math.abs(s.tx - s.x) > 0.05 || Math.abs(s.ty - s.y) > 0.05){
                anyMoving = true;
              }
            });
        
            rafTypoId = anyMoving ? requestAnimationFrame(tickTypo) : null;
          }
        
          // Activate magnetic mode AFTER intro (so word reveal animation finishes first)
          setTimeout(()=>{
            heroTitle.classList.add('magnetic-active');
          }, 4500);
        }
        
        // ============== HERO 3D — wireframe geometric structure ==============
        // 2.5D orthographic projection on canvas — no external libs.
        // Renders an octahedron with pulsing nodes that react to scroll & cursor.
        const hero3d = document.getElementById('hero3d');
        if(hero3d && !matchMedia('(pointer:coarse)').matches && !calm){
          const ctx = hero3d.getContext('2d');
          let W = 0, H = 0, DPR = window.devicePixelRatio || 1;
        
          function resize(){
            const r = hero3d.getBoundingClientRect();
            W = r.width; H = r.height;
            hero3d.width = W * DPR;
            hero3d.height = H * DPR;
            ctx.scale(DPR, DPR);
          }
          resize();
          // Cache the canvas rect so the mousemove handler below doesn't call
          // getBoundingClientRect() on every pointer move across the whole page.
          let hero3dRect = hero3d.getBoundingClientRect();
          function refreshHero3dRect(){ hero3dRect = hero3d.getBoundingClientRect(); }
          window.addEventListener('resize', ()=>{
            ctx.setTransform(1,0,0,1,0,0);
            resize();
            refreshHero3dRect();
          });
        
          // ---- Geometry: octahedron with secondary "test pyramid" inside ----
          // 6 vertices of octahedron (in unit cube)
          const baseSize = 130;
          const verts = [
            [0,  baseSize, 0],     // top
            [0, -baseSize, 0],     // bottom
            [ baseSize, 0, 0],     // right
            [-baseSize, 0, 0],     // left
            [0, 0,  baseSize],     // front
            [0, 0, -baseSize],     // back
          ];
        
          // Edges: every face of octahedron has 3 edges
          const edges = [
            [0,2],[0,3],[0,4],[0,5],  // top to mid ring
            [1,2],[1,3],[1,4],[1,5],  // bottom to mid ring
            [2,4],[4,3],[3,5],[5,2],  // mid ring
          ];
        
          // Inner test pyramid (3 layers — Unit / Integration / E2E)
          const innerSize = 60;
          const innerVerts = [
            [0,  innerSize*1.5, 0],          // peak
            [-innerSize, 0, -innerSize],     // base 1
            [ innerSize, 0, -innerSize],     // base 2
            [ innerSize, 0,  innerSize],     // base 3
            [-innerSize, 0,  innerSize],     // base 4
          ];
          const innerEdges = [
            [0,1],[0,2],[0,3],[0,4],     // peak to base
            [1,2],[2,3],[3,4],[4,1],     // base ring
            // mid ring at half-height
          ];
        
          // ---- State ----
          let rotX = 0, rotY = 0, rotZ = 0;
          let targetRotX = 0, targetRotY = 0;
          let mouseX = 0.5, mouseY = 0.5;  // normalized 0-1
          let scrollY = 0;
          let time = 0;
        
          document.addEventListener('mousemove', e=>{
            // Skip work entirely once the hero is scrolled out of view.
            if(scrollY > window.innerHeight) return;
            mouseX = (e.clientX - hero3dRect.left) / hero3dRect.width;
            mouseY = (e.clientY - hero3dRect.top) / hero3dRect.height;
            targetRotY = (mouseX - 0.5) * 0.6;
            targetRotX = (mouseY - 0.5) * -0.4;
          });
        
          window.addEventListener('scroll', ()=>{
            scrollY = window.scrollY;
            // Keep the cached rect accurate only while the hero is in/near view.
            if(scrollY < window.innerHeight) refreshHero3dRect();
          }, { passive:true });
        
          // ---- Rotation matrices ----
          function rotate(p, ax, ay, az){
            let [x, y, z] = p;
            // X rotation
            let cy = Math.cos(ax), sy = Math.sin(ax);
            let y1 = y * cy - z * sy;
            let z1 = y * sy + z * cy;
            y = y1; z = z1;
            // Y rotation
            cy = Math.cos(ay); sy = Math.sin(ay);
            let x1 = x * cy + z * sy;
            z1 = -x * sy + z * cy;
            x = x1; z = z1;
            // Z rotation
            cy = Math.cos(az); sy = Math.sin(az);
            x1 = x * cy - y * sy;
            y1 = x * sy + y * cy;
            return [x1, y1, z];
          }
        
          function project(p){
            // Simple orthographic projection with mild perspective
            const distance = 800;
            const factor = distance / (distance + p[2]);
            return [
              W/2 + p[0] * factor,
              H/2 + p[1] * factor,
              p[2]  // depth for z-sorting
            ];
          }
        
          // ---- Render loop ----
          function draw(){
            // Pause entirely when the page/window is hidden (e.g. you're working on the
            // other monitor). The visibilitychange handler below restarts the loop.
            if(document.hidden) return;
        
            // Scroll-driven fade. Check BEFORE clearing/repainting so that when the hero
            // is scrolled out of view we don't repaint the canvas at all — important over
            // DisplayLink, where every repaint is pushed across USB.
            const scrollFade = Math.max(0, 1 - scrollY / 600);
            if(scrollFade < 0.05){
              requestAnimationFrame(draw);
              return;
            }
        
            ctx.clearRect(0, 0, W, H);
        
            time += 0.005;
        
            // Smooth follow cursor + autonomous rotation
            rotX += (targetRotX - rotX) * 0.06;
            rotY += (targetRotY - rotY) * 0.06;
            rotZ = time * 0.15;
            const autoX = Math.sin(time * 0.5) * 0.15;
            const autoY = time * 0.2;
        
            // Transform all outer verts
            const projected = verts.map(v=>{
              const r = rotate(v, rotX + autoX, rotY + autoY, rotZ);
              return project(r);
            });
        
            // Draw outer octahedron edges (sort by avg depth — back first)
            const sortedEdges = edges.map(([a,b])=>{
              const za = projected[a][2], zb = projected[b][2];
              return { a, b, depth: (za + zb) / 2 };
            }).sort((p, q)=> p.depth - q.depth);
        
            sortedEdges.forEach(({a, b, depth})=>{
              const depthAlpha = 0.3 + (1 - (depth + 200) / 400) * 0.7;
              const finalAlpha = Math.max(0, Math.min(1, depthAlpha)) * scrollFade;
              ctx.strokeStyle = `rgba(212, 175, 55, ${(finalAlpha * 0.5).toFixed(3)})`;
              ctx.lineWidth = 1;
              ctx.beginPath();
              ctx.moveTo(projected[a][0], projected[a][1]);
              ctx.lineTo(projected[b][0], projected[b][1]);
              ctx.stroke();
            });
        
            // Draw outer vertices as nodes
            projected.forEach(p=>{
              const depthAlpha = Math.max(0.3, 1 - (p[2] + 200) / 400);
              const finalAlpha = depthAlpha * scrollFade;
              const radius = 2 + Math.sin(time * 2 + p[0]) * 0.5;
              ctx.fillStyle = `rgba(232, 203, 106, ${finalAlpha.toFixed(3)})`;
              ctx.beginPath();
              ctx.arc(p[0], p[1], radius, 0, Math.PI * 2);
              ctx.fill();
              // Glow
              const gradient = ctx.createRadialGradient(p[0], p[1], 0, p[0], p[1], 12);
              gradient.addColorStop(0, `rgba(212, 175, 55, ${(finalAlpha * 0.4).toFixed(3)})`);
              gradient.addColorStop(1, 'rgba(212, 175, 55, 0)');
              ctx.fillStyle = gradient;
              ctx.fillRect(p[0] - 12, p[1] - 12, 24, 24);
            });
        
            // Draw inner test-pyramid in oxblood
            const innerProjected = innerVerts.map(v=>{
              const r = rotate(v, rotX + autoX * 0.5, rotY + autoY * 0.5, -rotZ * 0.7);
              return project(r);
            });
        
            const sortedInnerEdges = innerEdges.map(([a,b])=>{
              const za = innerProjected[a][2], zb = innerProjected[b][2];
              return { a, b, depth: (za + zb) / 2 };
            }).sort((p, q)=> p.depth - q.depth);
        
            sortedInnerEdges.forEach(({a, b, depth})=>{
              const depthAlpha = 0.4 + (1 - (depth + 100) / 200) * 0.6;
              const finalAlpha = Math.max(0, Math.min(1, depthAlpha)) * scrollFade;
              ctx.strokeStyle = `rgba(122, 14, 14, ${(finalAlpha * 0.65).toFixed(3)})`;
              ctx.lineWidth = 1.2;
              ctx.beginPath();
              ctx.moveTo(innerProjected[a][0], innerProjected[a][1]);
              ctx.lineTo(innerProjected[b][0], innerProjected[b][1]);
              ctx.stroke();
            });
        
            // Inner peak vertex — bright accent
            const peak = innerProjected[0];
            const peakAlpha = scrollFade;
            ctx.fillStyle = `rgba(232, 203, 106, ${peakAlpha.toFixed(3)})`;
            ctx.beginPath();
            ctx.arc(peak[0], peak[1], 3 + Math.sin(time * 3) * 0.8, 0, Math.PI * 2);
            ctx.fill();
        
            requestAnimationFrame(draw);
          }
        
          // Activate after curtain fades
          let drawRunning = false;
          function startDraw(){
            if(drawRunning || document.hidden) return;
            drawRunning = true;
            draw();
          }
          // Mark the loop stopped whenever the page hides, and resume on show.
          document.addEventListener('visibilitychange', ()=>{
            if(document.hidden){
              drawRunning = false;        // draw() will return early and stop scheduling
            } else {
              startDraw();
            }
          });
          setTimeout(()=>{
            hero3d.classList.add('ready');
            startDraw();
          }, 1500);
        }
        
        // ============== SMART CURSOR (extended states) ==============
        // Add cursor states for code areas (text I-beam) and large elements
        const cursorRing = document.getElementById('cursorRing');
        // inject label span once
        if(cursorRing && !cursorRing.querySelector('.cursor-label')){
          const lbl = document.createElement('span');
          lbl.className = 'cursor-label';
          cursorRing.appendChild(lbl);
        }
        const cursorLabel = cursorRing?.querySelector('.cursor-label');
        
        function setCursorState(state, label){
          if(!cursorRing) return;
          cursorRing.classList.remove('is-text','is-large','is-hover');
          if(state === 'text') cursorRing.classList.add('is-text');
          else if(state === 'large') cursorRing.classList.add('is-large');
          else if(state === 'hover') cursorRing.classList.add('is-hover');
          if(cursorLabel) cursorLabel.textContent = label || '';
        }
        
        // Code areas → I-beam
        document.querySelectorAll('.ide-code, #archView').forEach(el=>{
          el.addEventListener('mouseenter', ()=> setCursorState('text'));
          el.addEventListener('mouseleave', ()=> setCursorState(null));
        });
        
        // Large clickable elements → expanded with label
        document.querySelectorAll('[data-cursor-label]').forEach(el=>{
          el.addEventListener('mouseenter', ()=> setCursorState('large', el.dataset.cursorLabel));
          el.addEventListener('mouseleave', ()=> setCursorState(null));
        });
        
        // ============== ARCHITECTURE VIEW TOGGLE ==============
        const ideCode = document.getElementById('ideCode');
        const archView = document.getElementById('archView');
        const archSvg = document.getElementById('archSvg');
        const ideTabs = document.getElementById('ideTabs');
        let archAnimated = false;
        
        function switchView(viewMode){
          const doSwitch = () => {
            if(viewMode === 'arch'){
              ideCode.style.display = 'none';
              archView.classList.add('show');
              ideTabs.style.display = 'none';
              // animate diagram first time only
              if(!archAnimated){
                archSvg.classList.add('animate');
                archAnimated = true;
              } else {
                // re-trigger animation each time arch view opens
                archSvg.classList.remove('animate');
                // force reflow
                void archSvg.offsetHeight;
                archSvg.classList.add('animate');
              }
            } else {
              ideCode.style.display = '';
              archView.classList.remove('show');
              ideTabs.style.display = '';
            }
          };
        
          // Use View Transitions API if supported, fallback otherwise
          if(document.startViewTransition){
            document.startViewTransition(doSwitch);
          } else {
            doSwitch();
          }
        }
        
        document.querySelectorAll('.view-toggle button').forEach(btn=>{
          btn.addEventListener('click', ()=>{
            document.querySelectorAll('.view-toggle button').forEach(b=> b.classList.remove('active'));
            btn.classList.add('active');
            switchView(btn.dataset.view);
          });
        });
        
        // ============== VIEW TRANSITIONS for FRAMEWORK TABS ==============
        // Wrap existing switchFramework to use view transitions
        if(typeof switchFramework === 'function'){
          const originalSwitch = switchFramework;
          switchFramework = function(fwKey){
            if(document.startViewTransition){
              document.startViewTransition(()=> originalSwitch(fwKey));
            } else {
              originalSwitch(fwKey);
            }
          };
          // Re-bind tab clicks to use new switchFramework
          document.querySelectorAll('.fw-tab').forEach(tab=>{
            const newTab = tab.cloneNode(true);
            tab.parentNode.replaceChild(newTab, tab);
            newTab.addEventListener('click', ()=> switchFramework(newTab.dataset.fw));
          });
        }
        
        // ============== SCROLL ==============
        // Custom wheel-based "inertia scroll" was removed — it hijacked mouse-wheel
        // events (preventDefault) and re-animated scrolling with a lag factor, which
        // caused visible stuttering on classic mice. Native browser scrolling is
        // smooth on its own, and `scroll-behavior:smooth` (set in CSS) still handles
        // anchor-link jumps.
        
        document.getElementById('year').textContent = new Date().getFullYear();
        </script>
